# Deviations from NetHack

Every intentional rule difference from NetHack is logged here.

| Area                                   | NetHack behavior                                                                                                                                                                  | Our behavior                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Reason                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RNG                                    | `rn2(x)` uses `RND() % x` (modulo bias)                                                                                                                                           | Rejection sampling, no modulo bias                                                                                                                                                                           | Uniform distribution; outcome sequences differ anyway (ref: src/rnd.c)                                    |
| Level generation                       | Locked doors and trapped doors (and door mimics)                                                                                                                                  | Locked doors are made with NetHack's rolls; trapped doors too (content keeps them in `modData.nh.doorTraps`, a secret door keeps its trap when found); from difficulty 9 a trapped (not secret) door is a doorway with a mimic posing as a closed door `!rn2(5)` of the time, unless all three mimics are genocided (m13 step 3); that roll and the mimic are made in content's trapped-door hook after the rooms and corridors, in core's door order, where NetHack makes them while making the door, so a deep level's later draws come in another order                                                                                                                                                    | Core makes the doors and has no monsters to make there (m13 plan decision 8) (ref: src/mklev.c dosdoor(); src/sp_lev.c create_door())                     |
| Level generation                       | MAXNROFROOMS is 40; MAXRECT is COLNO*ROWNO/30                                                                                                                                     | Room cap is `max(40, floor(40 * w*h / (80*21)))`; the rect cap already scaled with area                                                                                                                      | Large maps would otherwise stop at 40 rooms (ref: include/global.h, src/rect.c init_rect)                 |
| Level generation                       | dig_corridor gives up after 500 steps                                                                                                                                             | Cap is `max(500, 2*(w+h))`                                                                                                                                                                                   | 500 is too short for forced joins on very large maps (ref: src/sp_lev.c dig_corridor)                     |
| Level generation                       | Vaults, niches (`make_niches()`, some with a trap door), themed rooms, subrooms, special rooms are made; stairs depend on dungeon level (no upstairs on level 1)                                                           | Only ordinary rooms and corridors, plus random shops (6b3), throne rooms, leprechaun halls, zoos, temples, beehives, morgues, cockatrice nests, antholes and barracks (m12; see Random dungeon shops, Special rooms), vaults and the vault teleporter's niche (m12 step 6; see Vaults); no other niches; both staircases always exist (up in another room when possible, else a different cell of the same room)                                                                   | Not needed for M1 (ref: src/mklev.c makelevel, generate_stairs)                                           |
| Level generation                       | Rooms may end up disconnected (failed forced joins)                                                                                                                               | After makecorridors, any stray room is joined by a deterministic breadth-first corridor with plain doorways (no RNG)                                                                                         | Guarantees the "all rooms connected" invariant (ref: src/mklev.c makecorridors)                           |
| Level generation                       | Map is 79 usable columns (x = 0 unused), min size undefined                                                                                                                       | 0-based coordinates; internally x+1 is used so placement matches NetHack; minimum size 10x10 (smaller never places a room: create_room needs a 9-row free rectangle)                                         | Any size must work; NetHack's rect.c limits make smaller maps impossible                                  |
| Vision                                 | `clear_path()` Bresenham-style line-of-sight tables                                                                                                                               | Symmetric recursive shadowcasting; seen iff in LOS and (lit or adjacent)                                                                                                                                     | Symmetric, artifact-free, fast on large maps (ref: src/vision.c vision_recalc())                          |
| Light sources | `do_light_sources()` lights cells in a source's circle that `clear_path()` reaches from the source | The same circles (`circle_data`), but reach from a source off the hero's cell uses shadowcasting from that cell | Consistent with the hero's line of sight (ref: src/light.c do_light_sources()) |
| Doors                                  | Opening a door can fail ("This door is stuck") based on `rnl(20)` vs Str/Dex/Con                                                                                                  | Opening always succeeds, by walking into the door or with the Open action; a trapped door blows up when opened, unlocked, kicked open, dug through or struck by a wand ("KABOOM!!  The door was booby-trapped!", `b_trapped()` without exercise)                                                                                                                                                                                    | Attributes don't exist yet (ref: src/lock.c doopen_indir())                                               |
| Hero | Starting inventory by role and race: `ini_inv()` (random items filtered, race substitutions), the extras, known objects and classes, starting spells and gold, ammo in the quiver, the carry boost, `skill_init()`; `pauper_reinit()`, nudist, reroll and the wizard-mode Wishing kit; 5.0 makes it after `mklev()` and `makedog()` and draws `u.uhandedness` | Every kit, extra and race rule of u_init.c for all 13 roles and 5 races (m22 step 3; any of them can start a game since m22 step 8, see Roles and races), made in the `newGame` hook before the first level as 3.7 does, with 5.0's order inside it (HP and Pw, kit, attributes, carry boost, then use, skills and discoveries; plan Q9). Ammo and missiles stay in the pack (no quiver, see Firing); no pauper, nudist, reroll or Wishing kit; no `u.uhandedness` draw (nothing reads it); the Pw raise for a spell does not set `u.ueninc[1]` (pluslvl() overwrites it); no hidden gold is added to `u.umoney0` (starting sacks are empty); only types with a description are recorded as discovered (the others are known anyway), and none is marked encountered (no `oc_encountered`) | The port's game start order (ref: src/u_init.c u_init_role(), u_init_race(), ini_inv(), u_init_skills_discoveries(); src/weapon.c skill_init()) |
| Armor delay | `oc_delay` turns pass before the armor takes effect: AC and `known` change when `Armor_on` runs | The armor is worn and AC changes at once; the hero is then helpless for `oc_delay` turns (monsters act) with the same finishing message | Simpler state; the time cost is the same (ref: src/do_wear.c accessory_or_armor_on, Armor_on) |
| Armor I (m11 step 3) | 79 armor types; o_init.c shuffles the descriptions of four armor ranges (helmets, gloves, cloaks, boots); `Type_on()`/`Type_off()` give each armor's special effects (stealth messages, speed, levitation, fumbling, alignment...); `doread()` reads T-shirts, Hawaiian shirts and aprons (eroded text is wiped out); the bugle is one of many instruments (`do_play_instrument()` asks "Improvise?" and can play a tune for the drawbridge) | The 32 armor rows whose properties exist (mundane helmets, suits, shirts, cloaks, shields and boots; elven gear; cloaks of protection and magic resistance; helms of caution and telepathy; shields of reflection, drain and shock resistance; water walking boots), K- and C-rations ("This K-ration is bland.") and the bugle; the helmet, cloak and boots ranges are shuffled at new game after the classes (`ARMOR_RANGES`, the same partial Fisher-Yates over the range's descriptions as the classes); the gloves range waited for its gauntlets (step 4). Elven cloak and boots toggle stealth with C's lines ("You walk very quietly.", "You float imperceptibly.", "You sure are noisy.") and are learned then; a cloak of protection is learned when worn and gives MC 3 (also to a monster wearing one, `protects()`); the alchemy smock gives acid resistance with its poison resistance; the shield of drain resistance stops a wraith's AD_DRLI touch and the fire trap's level loss and shows in enlightenment (as C, not the angry god's drain); a monster wearing a cloak of magic resistance resists magic (`resists_magm()`); water walking boots keep her on top of water (m15 step 1: see Water; taken off on water she falls in, put on in it she rises, and they are learned then); the helms of telepathy and caution show their monsters at the next view refresh (no `see_monsters()`); reading a T-shirt or apron says "It reads:" and its slogan (by object id), a Hawaiian shirt its design (object id and the game seed for `ubirthday`), a shirt under a suit "Your shirt is obscured by your mail.", blind "You can't feel any Braille writing."; the text is never eroded and there is no literacy conduct. The bugle asks "Improvise?" (m15 step 8: a yes/no question that can be cancelled, "Never mind."; never asked when stunned, confused or hallucinating, who improvise); "no" asks "Play the passtune?" once she knows it, else the tune as a text prompt ("What tune are you playing? [5 notes, A-G]": spaces squeezed, letters raised, H read as B), "You extract a strange sound from the bugle!"; on the Castle the passtune next to the drawbridge teaches it and lowers or raises the bridge, a wrong one gives the tumblers and gears next to it ("You hear 2 tumblers click and 1 gear turn.", five gears teach it); no Wisdom exercise, achievements or Deafness. Improvising gives C's opening line by stun/confusion/hallucination, the jingle (`modData.nh.jingle`, kept while Unchanging), "You extract a loud noise from your bugle.", then every mercenary on the level wakes, turns hostile unless tame and "is now ready for battle!" (or "You hear the rattle of battle gear being readied." once), and other monsters within `u.ulevel * 30` wake, those within a third of it may flee (`awaken_scare()`, resisting at attack level 10); no Deafness, exercise, underwater, guards or strategies | Dragon scales and the other instruments do not exist yet (the instruments since m19 step 6: see Instruments); an eroded shirt's text would be the renderer's to wipe (messages are keys) (ref: src/o_init.c shuffle_all(), obj_shuffle_range(); src/do_wear.c Boots_on(), Boots_off(), Cloak_on(), Cloak_off(), toggle_stealth(); src/read.c doread(), tshirt_text(), hawaiian_design(); src/music.c do_improvisation(), awaken_soldiers(); src/eat.c fprefx()) |
| Armor II (m11 step 4) | Gauntlets of power (Str 25 in `acurr()`, +3..6 to a monster's weapon hits, -2 to hit with a bow), gauntlets of dexterity and the helm of brilliance (ABON by enchantment, `adj_abon()`), the dunce cap (Int and Wis 6, autocursed), the cornuthaum (Cha +1 and clairvoyance for Wizards, -1 and blocked for others; tourists can read the hat), speed boots (Very_fast), levitation boots, fumble boots and gauntlets of fumbling (the trip timer, `slip_or_trip()`, and Fumbling's other checks), the oilskin cloak, the cloak of displacement (`set_apparxy()`), the helm of opposite alignment (`uchangealign()`, the Furies when `rn2(50) < u.ualign.abuse`) | The 12 rows; the gloves range shuffles. acurr()'s overrides are applied to the stored attribute (ACURR) while the gloves or helmet are worn, the value they hide waits in `modData.nh.attrHeld` (written only then) and every attribute change goes to it meanwhile (`attrstate.ts`). The cornuthaum acts by role (m22 step 7) but cannot be read (tourists only). Speed boots make the hero Very_fast and a monster MFAST (`mcalcmove()`; a monster without permanent speed prefers them, `extra_pref()`). The Fumbling trip: C's `u.umoved` is the hero's last command moving her (kept in `modData.nh.fumbleFrom`/`umoved` only while the timer runs); she trips over the top object by its full name (never "it"/"them"), over her own feet, or stumbles; no ice, steeds or pools; she always hears "You make a lot of noise!". Fumbling also makes digging fumble (drop, bounce or bang) and fail on a bear trap more, makes kicks clumsy and kicked objects miss, stops a volley, keeps a seen trap from being escaped, doubles the untrap chance, sets off a box or door trap, and drops a wished-for object; not built: whips, grappling, jumping, cream pies, riding, the stairs fall, the door bump, autoopen, exercise, potion mixing. The helm of opposite alignment calls an erinys when `rn2(50) < u.ualign.abuse` (m18 step 4; no Astral Plane); priests do not wear it; prayer will not uncurse it. Cursed levitation boots or a stuck ring of levitation and cursed fumbling items are prayer troubles. The hero's grab slips off a monster's oilskin cloak (`m_slips_free()`). `adjattrib()` gives the dunce cap's "Your cap constricts briefly" only where the port shows the attribute message (C also for `msgflg` 0, the mind flayer's drain) | No roles, ice, steeds or pools yet; acurr() is not called on every read here (the stored attribute is what the status line and formulas read), so its overrides live where the value is stored (ref: src/do_wear.c Boots_on(), Gloves_on(), Helmet_on(), Cloak_on(), adj_abon(), toggle_displacement(), stuck_ring(); src/attrib.c acurr(), adjattrib(), uchangealign(); src/timeout.c nh_timeout(), slip_or_trip(); src/monmove.c set_apparxy(); src/mhitu.c u_slip_free(), wildmiss(); src/uhitm.c m_slips_free(); src/worn.c m_dowear_type(), extra_pref(); src/pray.c in_trouble()) |
| Eggs and #sit (m11 step 5) | Eggs hold a monster type (1 in 3 typed), hatch on a timer, can be laid by #sit as a female oviparous form; a pyrolisk egg explodes (fire, d(3,6)) when eaten, broken or hit with; `poly_obj()` turns a laid egg into another laid one; `unturn_dead()` revives carried eggs; genocide kills eggs (`kill_eggs()`); eggs hatching on another level do so when the hero returns ("hatched while away"); #sit covers thrones, pools, lava, ice, graves, ladders, drawbridges, steeds, hiding heroes and gremlins in fountains; thrown potions and glass break where they land (`throwit()`'s `breaktest()`) | The egg row, `mksobj_init()`'s typed-egg roll, `attach_egg_hatch_timeout()`/`hatch_egg()` (tame hatchlings from her own eggs, Its hissing sounds like "mommy.", MV_KNOWS_EGG in `modData.nh.knowsEgg`), `mergable()`, doname's "cockatrice egg" and " (laid by you)", wishing for "cockatrice egg", eating (stale "Ugh.  Rotten egg.", a cockatrice egg's delayed stoning), `dogfood()`, hitting with eggs thrown or wielded (`hmon_hitmon_misc_obj()`: "Splat!", a cockatrice egg stones the monster, an egg hitting a cockatrice becomes a rock), eggs breaking when thrown down, landing, kicked or struck (bad luck for her own), undead turning restarts a typed egg's timer, and #sit ("Sit" on the hero: swallowed, levitating, held, the top object, bear trap/pit/web, setting off the trap she sits on, sinks, altars, graves (m16 step 7), stairs, thrones (m12 step 2, see Thrones), laying eggs; m15 step 1: in the water (C's check of her boots wets her body armor, as C), on the muddy bottom, on lava and ice; no gremlin splitting or slime burning). Not built: the pyrolisk egg's explosion (it splats or is eaten like any egg), the laid-egg polymorph and `unturn_dead()` for carried eggs, and the #sit cases whose terrain or state does not exist. Global timers keep running while the hero is on another level, so such an egg hatches on its own turn, silently (no messages, no taming). Only eggs and (since m14 step 4) potions break where a thrown object lands; glass still lands whole. |
| Potions (m11 step 6) | The twelve potions of gain ability, restore ability, paralysis, speed, levitation, gain level, enlightenment, monster detection, gain energy, sickness, fruit juice and acid as `peffects()` has them; blessed monster detection gives a timed `Detect_monsters` ("You feel lonely." on an empty level); the taste of sickness and fruit juice names the player's fruit; a Healer is immunized; diluted potions and sinks; cursed gain level from level 1 needs the Amulet; the potions exercise attributes; acid and the ceiling bump honour Half_physical_damage; vapors and thrown potions (`potionbreathe()`, `potionhit()`) and monsters drinking them (muse.c) | All twelve are ported with their rolls and messages (see Levitation and Attribute loss for the levitation and AMAX parts), except: blessed monster detection acts as uncursed (one-shot map, cursed wakes the monsters) until the timed property exists; the taste is always "fruit juice" (no fruit option); no exercise or Half_physical_damage; `poisontell()`'s "innately weaker" wording; vapors and thrown potions came with m14 step 4 and monster use with m14 (see Monster item use) | No timed Detect_monsters, fruit option, exercise or Half_physical_damage (ref: src/potion.c peffects(), speed_up(); src/detect.c monster_detect(); src/apply.c unfixable_trouble_count()) |
| Scrolls and genocide (m11 step 7) | The scrolls of destroy armor, confuse monster, scare monster, create monster, taming, genocide, amnesia, fire and charging as `seffects()` has them; genocide also kills the hero's role monster (a confused reading names it, a cursed confused one sends some in), tracks extinction, turns chameleons, lists quest monsters' refusals, accepts class names ("dogs") and wizard mode's `*`; a monster or new monster is kept off a scary square by `goodpos()`/`enexto()` (GP_CHECKSCARY) as well as `mfndpos()`; Elbereth; `forget()` also clears `meverseen`; amnesia checks for a player named Maud; charging covers the rechargeable tools; shops charge for scrolls turning to dust and for altered objects; losing gloves while wielding a cockatrice corpse stones | All nine are ported with their rolls and messages, genocide (single, class, reverse, self and polymorphed self-genocide, the "Genocided species:" list, `makemon()`/`rndmonst()`/`mkclass()`, `polymon()`, `newcham()`, eggs, the arrival on another level) in `modData.nh.genocided`; the scare is a scroll of scare monster on the floor or an altar for vampires (`mfndpos()` ALLOW_SSM, `distfleeck()`, `rloc_pos_ok()`, the pet's retaliation), picked up it is marked, then turns to dust (here after the burden question). Not built: no quests (chameleons came with m18 step 3: one wearing a genocided kind's form takes another, a genocided shapeshifter dies in any form; extinction came with m18 step 1: a reverse genocide sends none of an extinct kind and stops with the last birth its limit allows); class genocide takes a symbol or a monster's name only; no `GP_CHECKSCARY` placement (Elbereth came with m16 step 5: see Engravings); `meverseen` does not exist and the player's name is never Maud; charging recharges the magic marker (once), tinning kit, horn of plenty, magic instruments, oil lamp and brass lantern as C since m19 step 1, and the tools that do not exist give no choice; no shop charges for these; no `selftouch()` for lost gloves | No shapeshifters, quests, engravings, the other chargeable tools or shop alteration costs yet (ref: src/read.c seffects(), do_genocide(), do_class_genocide(), recharge(); src/monmove.c onscary(); src/pickup.c pickup_object(); src/do_wear.c destroy_arm(), disintegrate_arm()) |
| Detection and the spellbooks (m11 step 8) | The potion of object detection, the scrolls of gold detection, food detection and magic mapping, and the 30 remaining spellbooks as `seffects()`/`peffects()`/`spelleffects()` have them: detection draws the found objects and traps on the map at once and lets the player browse it; buried objects, mimics posing as objects (for a cursed detector) and the steed count; trapped doors and chests are shown with their own trap glyphs; levels flagged `nommap` refuse magic mapping; the body-part words follow the form ("between your hooves"); clairvoyance at Skilled shows monsters as detected; blessed object detection and Skilled detect treasure mark everything seen up close; knock's push can stone a monster hurtled into a cockatrice; chain lightning (`cast_chain_lightning()`) | All of these are ported with their rolls and messages except: what is found goes into her object memory (and a floor trap's `seen`) and stays there until the cell is seen again (no `browse_map()`); a cursed object detector shows mimics posing as objects (m13 step 3); there are no buried objects or steeds; `nommap` levels (the Catacombs, the Valley of the Dead) refuse magic mapping as C's (m17 step 2: "Your mind is filled with crazy lines!", or for the spell "Your head spins as something blocks the spell!", and `rnd(30)` more turns of confusion; the head is always a head); a found chest trap is only made known (`tknown`) and a door trap is not drawn (no glyphs for them); the body parts are always "feet", "nose" and "toes"; clairvoyance remembers a monster she cannot spot as `I` (Skilled says "You sense your surroundings." when one was new); every object is seen up close anyway; `uedibility` is recorded but nothing reads it yet (edibility prompts are not ported); the spells that duplicate wands share the wand's code (force bolt .. cancellation, drain life on monsters and objects, finger of death, dig), fireball and cone of cold at Skilled ask where via the position prompt (`throwspell()`) and explode `rnd(8) + 1` times; knock pushes small monsters back (`mhurtle()` without regions, water or cockatrice touches); create familiar's pet type is C's kitten-or-little-dog roll; chain lightning is not ported (its row stays out) | No buried objects, steeds, trap glyphs for doors and chests, body parts of forms or edibility prompts yet; chain lightning in ROADMAP "From m11" (ref: src/detect.c gold_detect(), food_detect(), object_detect(), trap_detect(), do_mapping(), do_vicinity_map(); src/read.c seffects(); src/potion.c peffects(); src/spell.c spelleffects(), throwspell(); src/zap.c bhitm(), bhito(), zapyourself(); src/dothrow.c mhurtle(); src/dog.c make_familiar()) |
| Pick up | Autopickup per options; `pile_limit` option; menus ordered by class; `pickup_burden` option; scare monster / cockatrice corpse checks | Autopickup is off (NetHack 5.0 default); `pile_limit` 5 and `pickup_burden` Stressed are fixed; menu lists the pile top first; the cockatrice corpse check is ported (m9: `fatal_corpse_mistake()`, also when looting), the scare monster one is not; arriving by stairs prints no 'You see here' | Options and special objects are not implemented (ref: src/pickup.c pickup, lift_object, src/invent.c look_here) |
| Inventory display | Gold is `$` in the inventory; discoveries list in order of discovery with the bare description ('puce') | Gold is `$`; discoveries list in object-list order with the full appearance phrase ('puce potion', 'scroll labeled FOO') | Appearance names are whole phrases in the catalog (ref: src/o_init.c dodiscovered) |
| Wielding | Any item can be wielded; `x` swaps primary and alternate weapons (also with two-weapon combat); quiver | Only weapon-class items, weapon-tools and corpses (m9: a cockatrice corpse is a weapon; `cant_wield_corpse()` without gloves) are offered; the alternate weapon is content state (`modData.nh.uswapwep`, not a core equip slot) and `x` swaps as in `doswapweapon()` without two-weapon combat; wielding the alternate weapon swaps; no quiver; 'Wield nothing' is not offered once the wielded weapon is known to be cursed; `x` is offered only while a weapon is wielded or alternate | The quiver arrives with throwing; core has no alternate slot (ref: src/wield.c dowield, doswapweapon, setuswapwep) |
| Search | `dosearch0()` uses `rnl(7 - fund)` with Luck, lenses/artifact bonus, and also finds hidden monsters, mimics and traps | Secret doors and corridors, and traps (`!rnl(8)`, "You find a bear trap."; a statue trap goes off instead and ends the search: "You find a jackal posing as a statue."), with Luck and no lenses or artifact bonus; a found secret door becomes a closed or a locked door, as it was made; an explicit search finds a hiding monster next to her (m13: "You find a rock piercer.", `mfind0()`) and a disguised mimic (m13 step 2: "You find a giant mimic.") | Lenses and artifacts do not exist yet (ref: src/detect.c dosearch0(), mfind0(), find_trap()) |
| Luck | Luck is `u.uluck + u.moreluck`; luckstones and the full moon or Friday 13th set the base and stop timeouts; killing peacefuls, pets, humans ("You murderer!"), unicorns and quest monsters changes Luck and alignment | `u.uluck` with `change_luck()`, the 600-turn (300 when the god is angry) timeout toward 0, murder (-2), peaceful (-1 half the time) and pet (-1) kills; alignment: pets -15 ("distant thunder"), peacefuls -5, then the monster's `malign` (computed at the kill from its peacefulness); carried luckstones give `u.moreluck` (+3, -3 when cursed) and stop the timeout as in C (a blessed one keeps good Luck, a cursed one bad Luck, an uncursed one both); `u.moreluck` is worked out from the inventory whenever Luck is read rather than stored and updated by `set_moreluck()` (the same value, with no call site to miss); killing a unicorn of her alignment costs 5 Luck ("You feel guilty...", m13); no moon phases or quest monsters (temple priests: see Temple priests); the hero's state is `modData.nh.u` | No clock is read (determinism); those monsters do not exist yet (ref: src/attrib.c change_luck(), adjalign(), stone_luck(), set_moreluck(), src/timeout.c nh_timeout(), src/mon.c xkilled(), src/makemon.c set_malign()) |
| Prayer | `dopray()` also handles undead and demon forms, Gehennom, crowning, spellbook gifts, level drain, punishment, minions, reviving a pet's corpse on an altar (`pray_revive()`), the gnostic conduct, Wisdom exercise and wizard mode's "Force the gods to be pleased?" | The hero prays to her own god (her pantheon's, see "Pantheons and alignment"; Odin for the Valkyrie), or on an altar to the altar's god (Tyr, Odin, Loki or Moloch for the Valkyrie), with the confirmation and `can_pray()` (too soon, naughty, good; the record counts half on another god's altar and negated on the opposite god's, and a good prayer there is p_type 2), three helpless turns with invulnerability for a good coaligned prayer (monsters pull back, no hunger, regeneration or timeouts), `fix_worst_trouble()` for the troubles that can happen here, `pleased()` and `angrygods()` ("Thou durst scorn me?" on another god's altar). On an altar the favour roll is `rn1(Luck + 3 + on_shrine, 1)` without the off-altar cap of 3, so "fix every trouble" and the pat on the head come into reach; a good prayer on another god's altar costs a point of alignment and gives nothing else, not even a new prayer timeout. `water_prayer()`: a coaligned prayer blesses the water on the altar ("The potions on the altar glow light blue for a moment."), any prayer on another god's altar curses it (black), and a good water prayer there angers her own god as praying too soon does; an unaligned altar answers with Moloch's diabolical laughter (wakes the neighbours, -2 alignment, "Nothing else happens."). The castle-tune favor (m15 step 8) is C's ("Hark, mortal!" ... "To enter the castle, thou must play the right tune!", then "You hear a divine music..." with the notes) but becomes the golden glow when no tune was drawn (a game saved before m15) as it does once the drawbridge is opened or she has been in Gehennom (any of its levels: there is no Valley); in Gehennom (m15 step 9) a prayer is C's: no shimmering light or invulnerability, "Since you are in Gehennom, Odin can't help you." (her god) after it, and Moloch's anger (`angrygods()`) unless her alignment record is positive and `rnl(record)` is 0, also after Moloch's laughter; spell and crowning favors become the gift of an intrinsic, "relearn thy lessons" drains Wisdom but no level, punishment acts as the black glow and minions never come; corpses on the altar are left alone | Those systems do not exist yet; corpses do not remember the pet they were (ref: src/pray.c dopray(), can_pray(), prayer_done(), pleased(), water_prayer(), angrygods(); src/sit.c rndcurse(), attrcurse()) |
| Prayer troubles | `in_trouble()` also finds sliming, lava, clouds, walls, levitation, blindfolds, punishment, fumbling, saddles and deafness | Stoning (m9), lycanthropy (m9 step 7: "You feel purified."), strangling, sickness, Weak or Hungry, low HP, a welded weapon, cursed worn items (armor, amulet, rings), blindness, wounded legs, stun, confusion and hallucination; collapsing (Overtaxed with Str 4 below its peak) and lost attributes (m11 step 6: "You feel in good health again.") | The other conditions are not modelled (ref: src/pray.c in_trouble()) |
| Travel                                 | `findtravelpath()` falls back to guess mode (walks to the reachable cell nearest an unreachable target); `_` also offers `<` `>` shortcuts                                        | Unreachable/unknown targets are not offered and performing one costs no time; the path is re-planned every step over remembered map via `query.pathTo`                                                       | Simplification for M2 (ref: src/hack.c findtravelpath(), packages/content/src/travel.ts)                  |
| Travel | `lookaround()` also stops for fountains etc.; `context.travel1` first-step quirks | Travel stops on any cell with objects (via `check_here()`), and for hostile adjacent monsters; no other features or first-step quirks | Only objects exist (ref: src/hack.c lookaround, src/pickup.c check_here) |
| Melee combat: rings | Rings add to-hit/damage (u.uhitinc, u.udaminc), also for kicks and thrown objects | Worn rings of increase accuracy and damage add their enchantment (summed when used instead of kept in u.uhitinc/u.udaminc); to-hit uses Luck, Str/Dex, level, AC (worn armor), encumbrance, the target's sleep, stun and paralysis, wielded weapon enchantment/hit bonus and skill, -3 while the hero is in a trap and -2 for a trapped monster attacking her | Same totals; no other source of uhitinc/udaminc exists (ref: src/uhitm.c find_roll_to_hit(), hmon_hitmon_dmg_recalc(), src/dothrow.c thitmonst(), src/dokick.c kickdmg(), src/mhitu.c mattacku()) |

Naming one object (`do_oname()`): any carried object but gold, artifacts listed last; the current name is not offered as the default answer (C does that only with EDIT_GETLIN); an artifact "resists the attempt"; an artifact name the object may not have (`restrict_name()`, or the artifact exists) slips as C ("While engraving, your hand slips." / "You engrave: ..."), the count of scuffed letters from the cosmetic rng (C's display rng) and the letters from the game rng (as C's `wipeout_text()`); the right name on the right type makes the artifact; no illiteracy conduct or livelog | No monster names, floor objects, discoveries entries or level annotations yet (ref: src/do_name.c docallcmd, do_oname, name_ok, name_from_player, docall, objtyp_is_callable; src/artifact.c restrict_name; src/objnam.c xname) |

| Monster AI                             | `dochug()` also handles fleeing, scared monsters (Elbereth), confusion, invisibility, spellcasting, defensive/misc item use, weapon wielding, per-monster specials                | None of these but confusion, spellcasting (see Monster spellcasting), an invisible hero (see Monsters and an invisible hero), scared monsters (a scroll of scare monster, Elbereth: see Fleeing monsters and Engravings) and item use (healing potions, escapes by stairs and traps, defensive wands and scrolls, unicorn horn, lizard corpse, bugle, attack wands, thrown potions, misc items: see Monster item use): monsters hunt or wander (peaceful, 1/3 of bats), attack adjacent hostiles, and do nothing else; a confused or stunned monster (`modData.nh.mconf` / `mstun`) moves at random instead of attacking (it attacks when it did not move) and recovers 1 time in 50 (stun 1 in 10); confusion comes only from the confuse monster glow and a starving pet; a confused monster's `mfndpos()` gets ALLOW_TRAPS and ALLOW_SANCT but not ALLOW_U for a peaceful one or ALLOW_M, so it never stumbles into a fight with another monster or a peaceful one into the hero | No items or status effects yet (ref: src/monmove.c dochug(), m_move())                                    |

Tunnellers (dwarves) dig as C's `m_move()` does (ALLOW_DIG with a pick-axe, or a mattock without a shield; not when hostile within `dist2 <= 8` of the hero; peaceful ones keep out of shop and temple walls; a tunneller breaks the closed door it steps onto instead of opening it, with or without a pick, as in C), but tame ones never do: `dog_move()` asks no ALLOW_DIG; `m_balks_at_approaching()` is not ported, so monsters with launchers, polearms or a ranged attack (a pyrolisk's gaze) still close in instead of keeping their distance | The hero's trail does not exist yet; pets digging would need `dog_move()`'s postmov path (ref: src/monmove.c m_move(), postmov(), set_apparxy(), src/mon.c mfndpos()) |
| Monster item use (m14) | `find_defensive()`/`use_defensive()`: a monster near the hero that is badly hurt (or blind) drinks healing potions, flees up or down the stairs or the branch stairs, into a trap door or hole or onto a teleportation trap, up and out of the dungeon from the first level; it uses wands, scrolls, a unicorn horn, a lizard corpse or a bugle; `precheck()` may find a ghost in a milky potion or a djinni in a smoky one; ladders; Pestilence drinks sickness; Fort Ludios' `m_next2m()`; a monster that took the stairs comes out on the matching stairs and, after some time away, `wander`s up to 8 squares (somewhere in their room) | The healing potions (C's draws, threshold and overheal, "The hill orc drinks a potion of healing!", "looks better", blindness cured, the potion identified when seen) and the item-free escapes with C's checks and messages ("escapes downstairs!", "jumps into a trap door!", "escapes the dungeon!" with all it carries), the `m_flee` time, the cornered monster's escape in `m_move()` and `precheck()`'s cursed wand explosion (one time in 100, `d(spe + 2, 6)`) and, since m19 step 3, its ghost in a milky potion (the monster paralysed 3 moves) and djinni in a smoky one (peaceful or vanishing), each 1 in 13 + 2 * born; arrival on (or next to) the matching stairs, at a random spot when the level has none. Step 2: the unicorn horn (a non-cursed carried one, or a unicorn's own: "The tip of the white unicorn's horn glows!"), the lizard corpse eaten when confused or stunned (the meal takes the next move), a mercenary's bugle waking sleeping soldiers within 3 (`awaken_soldiers()` from a monster bugler), the wand of undead turning zapped at her when she wields a cockatrice corpse (C's `mbhit()`/`mbhitm()` for that wand: her carried corpses and those on the path rise, undead in the path resist or take `rnd(8)`), the wand of digging (a hole and the fall to the next level, a pit where the floor cannot be dug, "The digging ray is ineffective." on furniture), the wand of teleportation on itself, the scroll of teleportation (cursed or confused: `random_teleport_level()`, "shudders for a moment." on the same level), the wand and scroll of create monster (confused: acid blobs), with C's draws and the inventory loop's order; `mzapwand()` and `mreadmsg()`. An unseen scroll reader is "someone" or "something" (no `meverseen`) and an unknown label is read, not "incant"ed; pools (no eels or crocodiles from create monster), drawbridges or `trycall()`; no potion occupants, Pestilence, Ludios or Amulet (ladders came with m17 step 8: a monster flees up or down one, "The lich escapes up the ladder!", and comes out by the matching ladder); no `wander` on arrival (as no `mon_catchup_elapsed_time()` for migrating monsters). Step 3: `find_offensive()`/`use_offensive()` for the wands (death, sleep (not at a helpless hero), fire, cold, lightning, magic missile as rays from the monster, `buzz()` with BZ_M_WAND; striking, teleportation (when she is on a scary square, the stairs, a pile it wants, or behind a chokepoint with its friends near; not with teleport control) and undead turning (she carries a corpse, or one lies in line) through `mbhit()`/`mbhitm()`, striking breaking doors), C's early returns (peaceful, animal, mindless, handless, her sanctuary, the nurse, not lined up) and inventory order, the first attack-wand shot always missing (`mwandexp`), the ray wands skipped next to her or once it saw her reflect, the hook at the start of every `mattacku()` (an angry shopkeeper or priest zaps too) and "move then shoot"; the M_SEEN memory (`monstseesu()`/`monstunseesu()`: every monster that could see her learns or forgets) set where the port has C's sites: a ray hitting her (resisted or not, reflected or not) and the wand of striking's "Boing!"; the were creature's summoning comes after the wand here; no horns, camera, cancellation or Half_spell_damage; no level makes its monsters wand-experienced (no Castle, Ludios, Gehennom, Vlad's or quest); the other `monstseesu()` sites (spells, traps, potions, fountains, explosions, armor) do not teach the monsters, and only muse.c reads the memory. Step 4: the potions of paralysis (not at a helpless hero), blindness (not by a gazer), confusion, sleeping and acid (not once it saw her resist) hurled at her (`m_throw()`: "The hill orc hurls a potion of sleeping!"), breaking on her head or on a monster in the way (`potionhit()` with C's cases: healing kinds, sickness, confusion, invisibility, sleeping, paralysis, speed, blindness, water on undead, demons, were creatures, gremlins and iron golems, acid, polymorph) with the vapors (`potionbreathe()`) when on her or by chance next to her, the vapors of her own potions breaking next to her, of boiled potions she carries and of potions shattered in a forced box; the scroll of earth read next to her (a hard hat, confusion, a body boulders do not hurt, or 1 in 10): boulders around and on the reader ("The hill orc is killed." without credit) and on her. Not ported: oil, saddles, wet towels, a polymorphed gremlin hero splitting, `trycall()`, hallucinatory bottle names, the long worm tail's "body". Step 5: `find_misc()`/`use_misc()` with C's checks, draws and `nomore()` order, hooked in `dochug()` when `find_defensive()` finds nothing: a weak monster (difficulty below 6) jumps onto a polymorph trap next to it (not in iron shoes); the potion of gain level (`grow_up()`; cursed: up through the ceiling to the level above, or "looks uneasy."), the wand of make invisible and potion of invisibility (a cursed potion fails and `you_aggravate()`s her), the wand and potion of speed, the wand and potion of polymorph (into `rndmonst()`'s choice), the bullwhip pulling her weapon away (to the floor under it or her, into its hands, or slipping free) and taking things out of a carried container (`mloot_container()`). Not ported: shapeshifters (`cham`), dragon armor in `muse_newcham_mon()`, two-weapon combat and the heavy iron ball for the bullwhip, bags of tricks, ice boxes and Schroedinger's box, `trycall()`; `you_aggravate()`'s map flash; a further item taken from a container names the monster again ("The gnome removes ...": monsters have no gender, so no "He removes ..."). Step 6: `munstone()`, `munslime()` and `mon_reflects()` (see Stoning, Sliming and the reflection row); monsters put on amulets of life saving and reflection (`m_dowear()`; no amulet of guarding exists) and `lifesaved_monster()` saves a dying monster once ("But wait... The hill orc's medallion begins to glow!", hit points to at least 10; "Maybe not..." when the hero killed it unseen; a genocided one dies anyway); every death path checks the monster afterwards as C's `DEADMONSTER()` does, and the hero's kill gives no experience while it lives. A life-saved pet turns wary (`wary_dog()`) without C's `killed_by_u` (not kept: only its abuse counts); no vampire shapeshifters, disintegration or STRAT_WAITFORU. Step 7: monsters are made with C's items (see Monster starting gear), want what `searches_for_item()` lists (see Monster pickup) and pick up the corpses that cure stoning (and since m19 step 5 the lizard tins they could open); no horns, cameras or amulets of guarding to want | The potion occupants follow the hero's quaff (not ported), there are no Pestilence, Fort Ludios or monster-carried Amulet, and migrating monsters keep no last-move time (ref: src/muse.c find_defensive(), use_defensive(), precheck(), mon_escape(), searches_for_item(), mzapwand(), mreadmsg(), mbhit(), mbhitm(), mon_consume_unstone(), munstone(), munslime(), muse_unslime(), mon_reflects(), find_offensive(), use_offensive(), find_misc(), use_misc(), mloot_container(), you_aggravate(); src/potion.c potionhit(), potionbreathe(); src/dothrow.c breakobj(); src/dog.c mon_arrive(), wary_dog(); src/mon.c lifesaved_monster(); src/worn.c m_dowear(); src/music.c awaken_soldiers(); src/mondata.c monstseesu(); src/zap.c dobuzz(), zhitu()) |
| Boulders | `moverock()` also handles the air level ("You don't have enough leverage"), riding, very small and giant forms (stepping over, picking up), Passes_walls, tunneling monsters chewing, the `m` prefix ("There is a boulder in your way."), buried zombies, teleporters and level teleporters under the boulder, shop billing for pushed boulders and Str exercise, objects in a filled pit buried with it (`bury_objs()`), boulders dropped or thrown by monsters into a pit (`context.mon_moving`); the boulder's object class symbol is `` ` `` | Pushing, "the next boulder", the push-message throttle, "in vain" (walls, stone, boulders, closed doors, a diagonal into a doorway with a door), a monster on the other side (seen, or heard and remembered as `I`), squeezing onto a boulder when carrying almost nothing, the blind "That feels like a boulder.", holes and trap doors plugged, pits and spiked pits filled ("The boulder fills a pit."; a monster trapped in the pit does not stop the boulder and is hit by it as if the hero had thrown it, and is let out if it lives), a boulder pushed onto a rolling boulder trap rolls away ("The boulder suddenly rolls away from you!") toward the trap's far end or the edge of the map (NetHack 3.7's `moverock()`), a boulder pushed onto a land mine sets it off 9 times in 10 ("KAABLAMM!!  The boulder triggers a land mine."; the blast breaks it apart 9 times in 10, else it fills the new pit), a land mine's blast or other `scatter()` breaks a boulder lying there apart 9 times in 10, rolling boulders (see Traps: the hero; one set off by a monster hits a monster trapped in a pit it fills with `context.mon_moving`'s plain damage), boulders blocking sight and lying on top of their pile, running and travel stopping in front of one and travel never planning through two in a row, the hero's "carrying too much" diagonal squeeze, "You harmlessly attack a boulder.", kicking one ("Ouch!  That hurts!"; "There's not enough room to kick in here." while standing on one), boulders in extra corridors, the Mines filler and Orcish Town, random spots for new monsters and level contents skipping boulder squares (goodpos(), get_location(DRY)); the rest is not ported (Sokoban's rules: see Sokoban). A floating hero has not "enough leverage to push the boulder" (no squeeze either). Objects in a pit stay on the floor when a boulder fills it, and the objects in a pool a boulder fills stay there (nothing is buried). A boulder pushed, dropped, thrown or rolled into water fills it 9 times in 10 (a wall of water half the time, lava 1 in 10) or sinks (m15 step 3, `boulder_hits_pool()`; under a raised drawbridge it leaves floor under the bridge, m15 step 7; no Plane of Water yet). Pushing is always "With great effort" and never exercises Str. The boulder's glyph is `0` (NetHack's common `boulder` option) for readability on a phone | The hero is always a human on foot or floating, and the other systems do not exist yet (ref: src/hack.c moverock(), moverock_core(), dopush(), cannot_push(), test_move(), domove_fight_empty(); src/vision.c does_block()) |

A floating hero is "floating high above the fountain" (the drink is still offered: C's `dodrink()` does not ask while she cannot reach the floor; see Levitation); no enlightenment (its two messages only), "his"/"he" for the water demon, plain "snakes" and "water", no exercise | Those systems are not in the port (ref: src/fountain.c drinkfountain(), dowatersnakes(), dowaterdemon(), dowaternymph(), dogushforth(), dryup()) |
| Dipping | `dodip()` dips into a fountain, a sink, a pool or a potion (dilution, mixing, holy water, oil, the unicorn horn and amethyst), with `m` to skip the floor; a lawful hero's long sword may become Excalibur (Knights 1 in 6); poisoning weapons in sickness; `water_damage()` dilutes potions, destroys acid, washes off grease, wets towels and steams the Book of the Dead; `wash_hands()` cures Glib; naming the old potion after a unicorn horn changed it (`docall()`); dipping exercises Wis and logs the Lady of the Lake | `#dip` (`nh:dip`) is offered on every carried object but coins and armor or rings under other armor (C's `inaccessible_equipment()`), and "Wash your hands" (C's `-`) on herself where a fountain or pool is under her (elsewhere her hands could only be refused by the bottle); on a fountain or pool she can reach it asks "Dip X into the fountain?" first (no `m` prefix: "no" or Escape goes on to the potions, as C), then the menu of her potions; "Dip into" (`nh:dip_into`, C's #altdip) on a potion asks for the object. m19 step 4 ported `potion_dip()` whole: the Klein bottle, holy and unholy water (glows, BUC known only when seen, unpaid water repriced or billed), plain water, polymorph, mixing (`mixtype()`, 2 diluted / 3..8 magic / 7..N mundane potions, the explosion with the alchemy smock's 1 in 30, the random results, the mixture's colour, `hold_potion()`), the lichen corpse in acid, the towel soaking, acid corroding, oil (lit: fire damage; cursed: it spills; rust and corrosion away or an oily sheen on weapons; filling oil and magic lamps up to 1500, a lit one exploding), the unicorn horn and the amethyst, "Interesting...". dipfountain() is ported with every rnd(30) outcome, the bath (somegold() / 10) and the coins, and the Lady of the Lake (m20 step 2: a lawful hero of level 5 or more gets Excalibur 1 time in 30, a Knight 1 in 6; any other alignment gets the curse; the fountain goes and the Watch in town gets angry either way). Water damage blanks scrolls and spellbooks, dilutes potions (a diluted one becomes uncursed water) and explodes acid ("A potion of acid explodes!", "Another ..." in one soaking), rusts rustprone items, snuffs lit lamps (a carried lantern only flickers) and wets container contents (an oilskin sack or box keeps them dry); a cancelled potion of water is undiluted | No sinks, steeds, grease or poisoned weapons (sickness on a weapon is "Interesting..."); no `docall()` after a unicorn horn or amethyst (no `trycall()` anywhere); since m19 step 8 cursed oil makes her fingers slippery (Glib), a towel dipped in water gets wetter and washing her hands in a fountain or pool cures slippery fingers; no exercise, livelog or polypile conduct (ref: src/potion.c dodip(), dip_into(), potion_dip(), H2Opotion_dip(), mixtype(), dip_potion_explosion(), hold_potion(); src/fountain.c dipfountain(), wash_hands(); src/trap.c water_damage(), pot_acid_damage(); src/zap.c cancel_item()) |
| Holy and unholy water | `peffect_water()` exercises Con and Wis; a vampshifting hero hates blessings too | Every branch (m22 step 4): holy water burns an undead or demon form or a chaotic hero ("This burns like acid!", d(2,6), "Your affinity to werejackals disappears!" and the cure for a lycanthrope) and fills anyone else with awe (cures sickness and lycanthropy); unholy water heals the former ("You feel quite proud of yourself.", d(2,6); a lycanthrope in her own form turns), burns a lawful hero and fills a neutral one with dread; both leave the type unidentified. No exercise | Attribute exercise is not modelled; the hero is never a vampshifter (ref: src/potion.c peffect_water()) |
| Reading scrolls | `doread()` prompts to call unknown types, scores learning, and handles confusion, blindness, spellbooks, T-shirts and cookies | Scrolls are read as `flags.beginner` dictates with the confused and blind messages, but no naming and no score; `flags.beginner` is true until `modData.nh.urexp` reaches 2000 (the score is not tracked, so always true in practice), which is why "strange feeling" replaces "Your hands twitch." and "Your skin glows then fades." | Related systems missing (ref: src/read.c doread(), src/potion.c strange_feeling()) |
| Scroll of identify | A menu lists items that are not `not_fully_identified()`, which includes erodible items whose `rknown` is unset; Luck > 0 can add an item to a blessed scroll's count | Items are unidentified when BUC (or enchantment for weapons, armor, wands and charged rings) is unknown, the type is unidentified, or a container's contents or a box's lock are unknown; `rknown` is not part of the test (it is only recorded for erodeproof items); an empty answer re-asks up to five times like picking nothing in NetHack | No Luck; rknown is only kept where it changes a name or message (ref: src/read.c seffect_identify(), src/invent.c identify_pack(), menu_identify(), src/objnam.c not_fully_identified()) |
| Scroll of light | `litroom()` lights a radius-5 (blessed 9) circle around the hero, then hurts gremlins and light-sensitive monsters | The same circle is lit (cells the hero could see from her position, using our line of sight), and darkness douses the hero's lit lamps; gremlins and other light-sensitive monsters do not exist; confused light is skipped; on the Rogue level (m21 step 3) the whole room she is in, walls included, is lit or darkened as in C, and in a corridor nothing is lit and nothing said | No light-sensitive monsters or artifact lights (ref: src/read.c litroom(), src/vision.c do_clear_area(), circle_data[]) |
| Scroll of earth | `seffect_earth()` first checks for a ceiling (none in most of the endgame), words it as an "avalanche of boulders" in the quest, lets a hero who is engulfed hit her engulfer instead, and spares steeds; `drop_boulder_on_player()` spares polymorphed amorphous, unsolid and wall-passing forms and halves the damage with Half_physical_damage; `drop_boulder_on_monster()` also spares noncorporeal and wall-passing monsters; rocks dropped on the hero's own square at the edge of a pit tumble in, or fall through a hole; a monster's pronoun comes from its own gender | Read as in read.c: boulders (2..6 rocks each when confused) fall on the open squares around the hero (not closed doors, walls or rock) unless the scroll is cursed, and on her unless it is blessed ("But nothing else happens." when a blessed one drops nothing); a hard helmet caps the damage at 2 except against a cursed scroll; monsters hit are killed with her credit or woken and angered, and boulders fill pits and plug holes as pushed ones do; every level has a ceiling, called "temple's ceiling", "shop's ceiling", "ceiling" or, in a corridor, "rock cavern"; none of the excluded cases exist here; rocks on her square at a pit's edge just land there (no `ship_object()`); "does not protect him" takes the pronoun from the species (monsters have no gender of their own); reading it in Sokoban costs a point of Luck | No endgame, quest, engulfing, steeds, polymorph or Half_physical_damage (ref: src/read.c seffect_earth(), drop_boulder_on_player(), drop_boulder_on_monster(); src/dig.c ceiling(); src/trap.c sokoban_guilt()) |

The kit's corpses (the floor's, then hers) are one menu; nothing is felt blind; the tin opener's tin is chosen before it is wielded (cancelling takes no time) and a cursed opener is wielded without asking; slippery fingers drop the tin and greasy tins make them slippery as C (m19 step 8); no Riders; no `#tip`; no conducts | Menus replace getobj()'s letter prompts (ref: src/eat.c start_tin(), consume_tin(), use_tin_opener(); src/apply.c use_tinning_kit(); src/mkobj.c hornoplenty()) |

You will regret this!", Luck -2..-5, Aggravate_monster; a tainted one says ", you cannibal"), and a dog or cat corpse "was a bad idea" (Aggravate_monster); m13 step 8: a stalker corpse makes her invisible for `rn1(100, 50)` turns, or (already invisible) for good with see invisible ("You feel hidden!"), then stuns her twice as a yellow light does. Not ported: lycanthropy, polymorph, mimicking, level gain from wraiths, conduct; cannibalism by race and role since m22 step 5 (see Race rules) | No hallucination, conduct or special monsters yet (ref: src/eat.c cprefx, cpostfx, eatcorpse, eye_of_newt_buzz) |
