a doorway stands for both broken and missing doors, so locking one always says "A cloud of dust springs up and assembles itself into a door!"; Half_spell_damage, Deaf and steeds do not exist; death reasons read as NetHack's NO_KILLER_PREFIX ones ("zapped herself with a wand of X", "shot himself with a death ray" for a male hero); the death ray kills a living monster outright (no saving throw) unless it is a demon or resists magic (no Death or vampires; m15 step 6: a breathed death ray is the disintegration blast, `zhitm()`/`zhitu()`: disintegration resistance (also from worn black dragon armor) stops it, else it destroys the shield, else the suit and cloak, else the cloak and shirt and the target, a monster with what it carries but disintegration resistant armor, its amulet of life saving and 5 in 100 others, and no corpse; carried items that guard against it do not exist), kills her unless reflected or magic resistant ("You aren't affected."), and is drawn dark gray (CLR_BLACK); cancellation (`cancel_item()`) zeroes the enchantment or charges of magic objects (wands -1, not the wand of cancellation), blanks scrolls and spellbooks, turns potions to water (see invisible too: no fruit juice exists) and uncurses and unblesses, with shop billing ("You cancel that ..., you pay for it!"); `cancel_monst()` resists with resist(), cancels her inventory when she zaps herself (no message, as in NetHack) and returns a polymorphed hero to her own form (see Polymorph); no shapeshifters, mimics or clay golems; a cancellation beam blows up the magical traps it crosses (and the one under her zapped down) as `maybe_explode_trap()` does (polymorph traps too; no magic portals or vibrating squares exist, and no spell of cancellation); undead turning hurts undead `rnd(8)` (no vampshifters, no Knight's quest artifact doubling, no turn undead spell) and makes them flee unless they resist, revives the corpses carried by any monster it hits and the corpses it passes over ("The jackal corpse glows iridescently.", "The jackal is resurrected!"; a corpse left by an undead the same beam destroyed is skipped), and zapped at herself revives her carried corpses ("It suddenly comes alive!") before "You shudder in dread."; `revive()` makes a new monster of the corpse's kind at its spot or the nearest free one, one of a stack, with `eaten_stat()` HP for a partly eaten corpse and shop goods charged; not ported: eggs (nothing hatches), corpses in containers or buried, ghosts drawn back into their bodies, saved monster traits (a pet's corpse rises untame), corpse names, eels out of water, `mrevived` (no kill counts), and the corpses `cant_revive()` turns into a human zombie or doppelganger (aligned priests, unique monsters: neither monster exists, so they do not rise) | Related systems not ported yet (ref: src/zap.c bhitm(), bhito(), revive(), unturn_dead(), unturn_you(), dobuzz(), zhitm(), zhitu(), zap_over_floor(), probe_monster(), src/detect.c findit(), src/trap.c openholdingtrap()) |
| Enlightenment | `enlightenment()` titles the window "<Name> the <Role>'s attributes:", reports weapon and skill status, spellcasting penalties, polymorph, lycanthropy, steeds, being swallowed or held, water, invisibility, see invisible, infravision, half damage, teleport control, the hero's deaths so far and the real time played, with `from_what()` causes in wizard mode, and exercises Wisdom | The wand of enlightenment ("You feel self-knowledgeable...", the window, "The feeling subsides.") lists, under Status and Attributes, what the port models: levitating, strangled, food poisoning, nauseated, stunned, confused, hallucinating, blind (permanently, deliberately, temporarily), trapped in a trap, a wounded right leg, slippery fingers (m19), sleepiness, rapid hunger, the hunger state, encumbrance, wearing no armor; piety, magic protection and the resistances, telepathy, warning, searching, clairvoyance, adornment, stealth, aggravation, conflict, teleportitis, invisibility (invisible, invisible to others, visible), see invisible, levitation blocked by a bear trap, magical breathing, regeneration, slow digestion, to-hit, damage and defense bonuses, magic cancellation, reduced poison gas damage (a worn wet towel, m19), speed, reflection, free action, fixed abilities, life saving, Luck, luckstones, god anger or whether she can safely pray; the window title is "Your attributes:" (no hero name exists) and there is no Miscellaneous section (real time would break determinism); no exercise | Subset of the modelled state (ref: src/insight.c enlightenment(), status_enlightenment(), attributes_enlightenment(); src/zap.c do_enlightenment_effect()) |
| End of game | really_done() asks the disclosure questions (`disclose` option: possessions, attributes, vanquished, genocided, conduct and achievements, dungeon overview) as game prompts, may show what a shopkeeper took (`taken`), writes a dumplog and bones, and the final attributes say "You were ..."; the score adds artifact points, the escaping pets' hit points and the ascension bonus, and subtracts the starting gold (`u.umoney0`); the vanquished list has a sort-order menu ('a') and pads its lines to align the counts | The pack is identified in the game (C's loop) and the lists are game events; the questions are asked by the UI (Yes / No / Quit) and never enter the replay. Possessions (container contents included), attributes and creatures vanquished (every monster death counts, as C's `mvitals[].died`; C's default order, monster level high to low; plurals by the UI's `{x|plural}`, a makeplural() port) and the genocided species (m11 step 7) with the extinct ones (m18 step 1: " (extinct)" lines, "N species extinct.", the question and title by what is listed) are disclosed; no conduct, achievements or overview; no `taken` variant, Schroedinger's cat, dumplog or bones. The final attributes keep enlightenment's present tense ("You are ...") under the title "Final attributes:". The stone's name is the device's name option (asked on the title screen's Play), else the role's name; a death text's article follows the port's killer keys (monsters get one, `killer.*` phrases are worded whole). The score (`u.urexp`: four times each experience gain, +10 for a scroll, potion or wand type learned by using it) and the final points (net gold, containers included, less a tenth unless she escaped or quit; 50 per level below the first; 1000 per level past 20, at most 10; an escape adds carried amulets and gems at base cost) are content statuses after game over, never stored; no artifacts, pets' hit points or ascension; the starting gold is taken as 0 (the Valkyrie starts with none; a mod role with starting gold would score it as gained); the vanquished lines are not padded and there is no sort-order choice | The answers change nothing in the game; nothing tracks conduct or the overview yet; enlightenment.ts words many lines whole, so no single you_are() helper could take C's `final` flag (ref: src/end.c really_done(), disclose(), get_valuables(); src/exper.c more_experienced(); src/insight.c enlightenment(), list_vanquished(); src/topten.c formatkiller(); src/objnam.c makeplural()) |
| High scores | topten() keeps a shared `record` file of `sysopt.entrymax` entries, at most `persmax` per name and role ("You didn't beat your previous score"), points below `pointsmin` dropped; after the game it shows the top `end_top` (3) and `end_around` (2) lines around the new entry (or `end_own`), wrapped and padded to 80 columns; also writes `logfile`/`xlogfile` | A per-device list in localStorage (`nh.topten`, top 100, never in saves), no per-name limit, games with 0 points not recorded; shown from the end sheet, the Menu and the title screen: the top ten plus the new entry, standing out, as rows that wrap (rank and points left, hit points right); entries keep the end's words as rendered then, plus seed, date and app version; no log files; wizard-mode games (the `wiz` mod loaded) are not recorded, as C | No shared file or server on a static site; a phone screen fits the top ten better than C's padded columns; options for `end_top`/`end_around` do not exist yet (ref: src/topten.c topten(), outentry(), outheader(); src/end.c really_done()) |
| Options | Options are set in a run-time configuration file or the `O` menu: `name` (asked as "Who are you?" at start), `number_pad`, `runmode` (default `run`: the display updates every 7th step; `teleport` only when she stops; `walk` every step; `crawl` with four extra delays), `disclose` per list (`i a v g c o`, each ask with default yes or no, always, or never), and many more | A per-device Options sheet (Menu > Options, localStorage `nh.options`, never in saves): the name (at most 31 characters, used on the stone, in Goodbye and on the score list; the game itself still greets "Valkyrie"), the movement keys (number pad or vi keys; reloads), the run speed (default `walk`, today's pace; `run` spreads its one delay per 7 steps evenly over them; `teleport` has no delay but the browser may still draw some steps; thrown objects fly at the same pace whatever the run speed) and what the end of the game discloses as one setting for every list (ask, show every list, show nothing), plus resetting the zoom | Client-side presentation only, so replays stay deterministic; game-side options are a later milestone; a phone sees each step better at `walk` (ref: src/options.c optfn_runmode(); src/hack.c runmode_delay_output(); src/end.c should_query_disclose_option()) |
| Quit and new game | `#quit` asks "Really quit without saving?" (paranoid_confirmation:quit; a tutorial question first; wizard mode then asks "Dump core?"), then done(QUIT); a hero at 0 hit points who quits dies ("quit while already on Charon's boat"); a new game asks "Who are you?" unless `name` is set, then the role, race, gender and alignment | `#quit` is a self action (the Actions menu, last; no key, as C's default table) asking "Really quit without saving?" (yn, default no); yes ends the game as `quit` through the same disclosure and summary as a death, with no stone and every piece of gold counted; no tutorial, no "Dump core?", no Charon's-boat case (a hero at 0 hit points never gets to act). With no game to resume the app opens on a title screen (Play, Wizard mode, High scores, Help, Options); Play asks "Who are you?" only while the device has no name (a blank answer asks again), then the character (see Choosing a character); Menu > New game during a game goes through `#quit` | A browser game has no command line to start from; the name is a device option, not game state (ref: src/end.c done2(), done(); src/cmd.c extcmdlist[] "quit"; src/role.c the name prompt) |
| Digging | Pick-axes, mattocks and dwarves dig; the wand and spell of digging zap through walls, doors and rock, down (`dighole()`: holes, pits, liquid flowing into the hole, buried objects unearthed, a magical trap exploding, drawbridges and graves dug up, thrones and altars refused) and up; from inside a pit a lateral zap digs one adjacent pit and conjoins them; digging in a shop costs `pay_for_damage()` (and `shopdig()` when she falls through); objects on the square fall with her (`impact_drop()`); Levitation and Flying keep her up | The wand of digging only (`zap_dig()`): across the level `rn1(18, 8)` squares, closed and secret doors razed to empty doorways ("The door is razed!"), room walls to doorways (corridor on cavernous Mines levels outside town), rock to corridor, trees to floor; on maze levels (Sokoban, Mines' End, Grotto town) the first wall or rock becomes floor or corridor and stops the beam, or "The wall glows then fades." / "The rock glows then fades." where it is non-diggable; elsewhere a non-diggable square just stops it; down: a hole she falls through to the next level ("You dig a hole through the floor.", "You fall through...", `d(1, 6)`), or a pit (`rn1(4, 2)` turns) where the floor is hard or at a dungeon's bottom (Sokoban's entry level, Mines' End; the main dungeon has none), "The floor here is too hard to dig in." in a pit there, a fountain dries up, a sink breaks into a fountain, thrones and altars refuse ("The throne is too hard to break apart."), a grave refuses as hard ground ("The headstone here is too hard to dig in.": digging graves up is not built, m16 step 7; a pick and a broken wand refuse it too), a boulder falls in ("KADOOM!"); on stairs or up: a rock falls on her head (`rnd(6)`, `rnd(2)` with a hard helmet). Dug pits and holes are hers (`madeby_u`). The pick-axe and dwarvish mattock (`#apply`, or the "Dig" offer on an adjacent square it would dig into and "Dig down" on herself while she carries one): applying wields it first (that takes the move; the direction is asked on the next, as C queues the apply again), then the direction (all eight, herself, up and down are always listed); `dig()` is a resumable occupation ("You start digging." / "You continue digging." on the same square and level, the effort kept; any other action or a hostile monster next to her stops it, "You stop digging."), effort `10 + rn2(5) + abon() + spe - erosion + udaminc` a turn: sideways over 100 rock becomes corridor, a room wall an opening (floor on maze levels, corridor on cavernous ones outside town), a secret or closed door is broken, a boulder falls apart, a statue shatters ("You start chipping the statue.", `break_statue()`: what it held drops; "You can't reach the statue." from a pit as for boulders); non-diggable walls are "too hard to dig into"; a shop's walls and door "seem too hard" on each swing that does not break through (the effort still adds up, and breaking through is charged); a pick against a tree needs an axe; down: a pit after 50 (the dig then ends; applying again goes on to the hole after 250, and she falls through), stairs, thrones and altars refused, a bear trap holding her hit, other traps destroyed where the floor cannot be dug through. From inside a pit: a lateral zap digs one pit on the next square (rock there becomes corridor; walls, doors, trees, bars, sinks, fountains, thrones, altars and stairs refuse with `adj_pit_checks()`'s messages) and conjoins the two, passing over a pit already conjoined that way as C's loop does; a swing at a seen pit next to hers that is not conjoined "clears some debris from between the pits" (conjoins them; the pick never digs a pit there, as in C), else "the rubble has no place to go"; the pick reaches a boulder in a conjoined pit; a pit filled, dug out or removed loses its links (`clear_conjoined_pits()`). Dwarves tunnel (`mdig_tunnel()`): the closed, locked or secret door they step onto is broken ("You feel an unexpected draft." one time in three), a secret corridor opened ("You feel a draft."), a wall becomes an opening (floor on maze levels, corridor on cavernous ones outside town; "You hear crashing rock." one time in five), a tree floor, rock corridor with a rock (3 in 12) or a boulder (1 in 12) left; non-diggable walls (Sokoban, Mines' End) stop them. In a shop (see Shop damage): walls, doors, pits and holes are damage the keeper repairs, hers to pay for (a dwarf's are free); digging down there gets "Madam, do not damage the floor here!" and falling through owing him costs her pack. A statue on a statue trap comes to life instead of shattering. Drawbridges (m15 step 8): digging down on a lowered drawbridge or at its portcullis destroys it ("The drawbridge seems too hard to dig through." for a pit only), on a raised span over ice or floor the hole fills from the moat or lava around it and the bridge stays over it ("As you dig, the hole fills with water!"), else the ground is too hard; a broken wand's or a dug pit's hole at a lowered bridge destroys it too (`furniture_handled()`); a pick against the raised portcullis: "This drawbridge is too hard to dig into."; a monster's defensive wand of digging is ineffective on a drawbridge. Not ported: axes (applying one, chopping trees, `rnd_treefruit_at()`: a tree a dwarf digs leaves no fruit), Fumbling, the Earth level, burying, liquid flowing into a pit dug sideways from a pit (`pit_flow()`), `impact_drop()`; no pits or holes in doorways ("The doorway here is too hard to dig in.": traps stay on plain floor; m17: a monster standing in a doorway does not zap digging down, `find_defensive()`); applying a pick at a pet or peaceful she sees draws do_attack()'s rolls, then digs past it | Subset; core keeps traps on plain floor (ref: src/dig.c zap_dig(), dighole(), digactualhole(), dig_check(), furniture_handled(), watch_dig(), use_pick_axe(), use_pick_axe2(), dig(), dig_typ(), adj_pit_checks(), mdig_tunnel(); src/trap.c conjoined_pits(), clear_conjoined_pits(); src/dungeon.c Can_dig_down(); src/hack.c may_dig(); src/mkmap.c mkmap(); src/wield.c wield_tool()) |
| Monster speed | `mon_adjust_speed()` also handles speed boots, petrification and green slime; `permspeed` and `mspeed` are separate fields | `modData.nh.mspeed` holds MSLOW/MFAST for wand-slowed or hasted monsters (no speed boots, so mspeed = permspeed); `mcalcmove()` applies it | MonsterComponent is core's; no speed boots (ref: src/worn.c mon_adjust_speed(), src/mon.c mcalcmove()) |
| Tools | Many tool types; `doapply()` also handles candles, the magic lamp, bags of tricks, ice boxes, mirrors, horns, leashes and more; tools and their descriptions are part of `o_init.c` (no tool shuffle) | Since m19 step 1 also the magic lamp, towel, tinning kit, tin opener, magic marker, the wooden and magic flute, tooled, frost and fire horn, horn of plenty, wooden and magic harp, leather drum and drum of earthquake: generated with `mksobj_init()`'s charges and the lamp's djinni, named ("magic marker (0:50)", "moist towel"), wished for, priced, stocked (the lighting shop's magic lamps), polymorphed into (a magic lamp comes out an oil lamp, a marker recharged once) and recharged (markers once, kits, the horn of plenty, the magic instruments, and the oil lamp and lantern), and all of them applied as NetHack: candles, the magic lamp and potions of oil light (step 2, see Lamps); the tinning kit, tin opener and horn of plenty (step 5, see Tins); the instruments (step 6, see Instruments); the magic marker (step 7, see Magic markers); the towel (step 8, see Towels and slippery fingers). Since m19 step 3 `#rub` (its own action, Alt+r) rubs a lamp, the lantern or royal jelly as NetHack's `dorub()` (wielding the lamp first, the magic lamp's djinni, royal jelly on eggs); the gray stones are not rubbed (no touchstone). The 18 tools of the subset before them (large box, chest, sack, oilskin sack, bag of holding, skeleton key, lock pick, credit card, brass lantern, oil lamp, blindfold, tin and magic whistle, the bugle since m11 step 3 (see Armor I), land mine, beartrap, pick-axe, and since m13 the unicorn horn (a weapon-tool, never generated: see Unicorns)) with Apply offered on each carried one (and on the dwarvish mattock); the pick-axe is a weapon-tool (wielded with `w`, its enchantment and erosion shown, welded when cursed); tools are generated, boxes and bags with `mkbox_cnts()` contents (no gems: a gem roll adds nothing); boxes are trapped by the `otrapped` roll (see Containers); an oilskin sack behaves like a sack (it only differs in water, which does not exist); the land mine and beartrap objects (never generated) come only from `#untrap` and wishes, and cannot be applied: setting them (`use_trap()`) is not ported | Subset: the Bell of Opening, Candelabrum, mirror, crystal ball, lenses, can of grease, grappling hook, ice box and bag of tricks are not ported (ROADMAP "From m19"); the bell, expensive camera, saddle, leash and stethoscope came in m22 step 2 (see Kit tools) (ref: src/apply.c doapply(), include/objects.h) |
| Kit tools (m22 step 2) | The stethoscope, expensive camera, leash, saddle and bell, the fortune cookie, orange and pancake; `use_stethoscope()` (one free use per move by `hero_seq`, Deafness, underwater and steeds), `use_camera()` (`bhit()`'s flash, `see_monster_closeup()`), `use_leash()` with `check_leash()`, `next_to_u()` pulling a leashed pet next to her before she leaves and `dog_move()` dragging it back, a cursed leash holding her back; `use_saddle()` and riding; `use_bell()`; the cookie's `outrumor()` and the literacy conduct | All eight rows as objects.h (random pools, prices, `mksobj_init()`'s camera charges, recharging and the camera's usage fee) and the golems' leashes and saddles. The stethoscope as C, without Deafness, underwater or steeds (hero_seq is the turn plus her movement points left); the camera as C, the flash going through invisible monsters, a cursed one or one aimed at herself blinding her for 5 + rnd(25) (no gremlin damage); the leash as C (two at most, "You slip the leash around your kitten.", " (attached to the kitten)", it cannot be dropped or put in a container, the pull, choke and snap as she walks away, a leashed pet kept within distance 2 in `dog_move()`, "leash goes slack" / "suddenly comes loose" when she leaves without it, "pulls free" when it is untamed, "breaks loose" when it attacks her, the status line's "leashed"); the bell as C's ordinary bell (the cursed nymph, shattering, the nymph's speed or her stun); the cookie's fortune (true when blessed, false when cursed; never rotten; blind: "What a pity that you cannot read it!") | No dragging (plan Q10): a leashed pet left more than a square away by a teleport or a trap stays put until she comes back or the leash snaps, and a cursed leash never holds her back from stairs or a teleport; the saddle cannot be applied (no riding, ROADMAP); stolen or destroyed leashes, dead pets and pets taken off the level are let go at the end of the turn, a pet carried off the level with "Your leash goes slack."; no hallucinatory whimpers (ref: src/apply.c use_stethoscope(), its_dead(), use_camera(), use_leash(), use_leash_core(), check_leash(), use_bell(); src/dogmove.c dog_move(); src/dog.c keepdogs(), abuse_dog(); src/eat.c fpostfx(); src/rumors.c outrumor(); include/objects.h) |
| Pantheons and alignment (m22 step 4) | `role_init()` draws a Priest's pantheon (`randrole(FALSE)` until a role with gods) before the dungeon is laid out; the alignment is the player's choice; `u.ualignbase[A_CURRENT]` changes on altar conversion; `halu_gname()` gives a hallucinating hero random gods for priests and minions; `align_gtitle()` (god or goddess) feeds the quest texts | Every role's gods, with C's goddesses, through `alignGname()`/`alignGtitle()` (god names in prayer, altars, temple priests "priest of Athena", the god's voice and anger, "the wrath of Huhetotl"); a Priest draws the same way in the `newGame` hook, after the layout, and the pantheon is stored (`modData.nh.pantheon`) only when it is not her own role's. At new game the alignment is the character's (m22 step 8; by default her role's first one that her race allows) and the record is the role's `initrecord` (10 for Archeologists, Barbarians, Healers, Knights, Monks, Rogues, Rangers and Samurai), with the original alignment (`alignBase`, stored only when not neutral: older saves are all neutral Valkyries); a helm of opposite alignment taken off returns her to it. | Race rules (m22 step 5) | The hero's race decides who is always peaceful or hostile (`race_peaceful()`/`race_hostile()`), cannibalism, racial food, polymorph and genocide of her own kind, volleys and bow skill, digging, the shopkeeper's "hiril"; her own form (`set_uasmon()` unpolymorphed) is her role's monster with its properties, and her infravision comes from her race's monster; a dwarf has "earth sense" (`earth_sense()`: "You sense something below your feet." on arrival over a buried object); a few more `Race_if()` lines (the wail at low hit points, the orc smell, the touchstone, cutting down a tree, the mummy or zombie she rises as) | The race rules of the ported functions (peace_minded(), makemon()'s S_ORC, create_monster()'s Mines kin, maybe_cannibal() whole with the polymorphed and lycanthrope cases, adj_victual_nutrition(), fprefx(), the cursed apple, newuhs()'s "Elf needs food, badly!", newman() and rehumanize() texts, Your_Own_Race in single and class genocide, ugenocided(), throw_obj()'s volleys and weak roles, thitmonst()'s and find_roll_to_hit()'s elf-against-orc bonus and the bow skill, the elven arrow's and ya's extra damage, dig(), append_honorific(), domonnoise()'s MS_ORC). Her own form gives only her race's infravision (a set_uasmon() stand-in): no property of her role's monster (a Valkyrie's cold resistance comes from her intrinsics table); for domonnoise() her own form is a plain human. Not ported: earth sense (its `u_on_newpos()` call sites are spread over ten files), and the lines whose features the port lacks (`maybe_wail()`, the monster-arrival smells, touchstones, axes, bones, `#offer`'s own-race sacrifice, `meverseen`) | The race rules are content (ref: src/makemon.c peace_minded(), makemon(); src/sp_lev.c create_monster(); src/eat.c maybe_cannibal(), adj_victual_nutrition(), fprefx(), fpostfx(), newuhs(); src/polyself.c set_uasmon(), newman(), rehumanize(), ugenocided(); src/read.c do_genocide(); src/dothrow.c throw_obj(), thitmonst(); src/uhitm.c find_roll_to_hit(), hmon_hitmon_weapon_ranged(); src/dig.c dig(); src/shk.c append_honorific(); src/sounds.c domonnoise(); src/dungeon.c earth_sense()) |
| Role rules in combat (m22 step 6) | Monks and Samurai fight with martial arts (`martial_bonus()`: rnd(4) bare-handed, doubled to-hit and tripled damage skill bonuses, "martial arts" in `#enhance` up to Grand Master, the kick's `martial()`); an unarmed blow may stagger the monster; skilled bare hands punch twice; a Monk's suit costs to-hit and fighting without weapon and shield helps; the Knight's "You caitiff!" and the Samurai's "You dishonorably attack the innocent!" and "You dishonorably use a poisoned weapon!"; the Rogue's backstab; the Healer's knife; the roles' volley bonuses; the Knight's knight's move | Ported: `find_roll_to_hit()`'s Monk lines and martial kick, `check_caitiff()` from melee (once per round), kicks, thrown objects and applied polearms, `missum()`'s "Your armor is rather cumbersome...", `double_punch()` (the second blow's to-hit takes the alternate weapon's `hitval()` and skill bonus, as C passes `uswapwep`), `hmon_hitmon_barehands()`, `hmon_hitmon_stagger()` with `mhurtle()`, the 3/4 Str bonus of two punches, the Rogue's backstab in melee and with an applied polearm, the Healer's `min(3, died / 6)` with a knife, `weapon_hit_bonus()`/`weapon_dam_bonus()`, `P_NAME()`, `kickdmg()`'s martial-arts training, `multishot_class_bonus()`; the Knight's jumping was already there (kit.ts, jump.ts). Uncontrolled level teleports (a cursed or confused scroll): depth 1 to current + 3 (the current depth 1 time in 5: "You shudder for a moment."), no deeper than the dungeon's bottom (the main dungeon's last level, the Castle; level 25 in a game saved before m15, as for holes, see Dungeon bottom), a depth above a branch dungeon going to its parent (`get_level()`), "You materialize on a different level!" on a random spot. A controlled level teleport (the cursed or confused scroll, a level teleporter) asks "To what level do you want to teleport?" in a text prompt: a depth, "*" for a random one, a confused hero's "Oops..." (rnl(5), even when she cancels), text that is no number asks again ("[type a number]": level names are not taken) up to 10 times then goes at random, her own depth does nothing, past the bottom is the bottom, except that past the main dungeon's it is Gehennom's first level (`find_hell()`, m15 step 9); 0 asks "Go to Nowhere. (yes is suicide; life saving rematerializes her), below 0 is the sky: heaven (death) at -10 or less, Cloud 9 at -9, floating down to earth (escaping the dungeon) when levitating, else a fatal fall (life saving leaves her on the surface, escaped); a level teleporter then says "You briefly feel centered." and adds no confusion. The wand teleports monsters (`rloc()`: 50 random tries, then every square shuffled; "The jackal vanishes and reappears close by."; a priest in a temple resists; a resident shopkeeper or priest stays in his room when he can, and a shopkeeper taken out of his shop gets angry; shop goods a monster carries out are stolen) and objects (`rloco()`: a boulder landing on a pit or hole fills it; shop goods leaving the shop floor are stolen unless she is in the shop and they land in it, or on its boundary, where they go on her bill). `+` can reorder and sort spells | `Z` (the Cast offer on herself, once she knows a spell) always shows the menu (letter, name, level, school, fail rate, retention) and can be dismissed at no cost; `rejectcasting()` (stunned, strangled, no free hand unless a quarterstaff), `spelleffects_check()` (forgotten spells backfire, the four fading warnings, too hungry, too weak, overloaded, "yet"/"anymore", the hunger cost capped at 3 nutrition left, the failure roll at half the energy), energy and `use_skill()` as in spell.c; light, detect monsters, cure blindness and protection (the golden haze, its AC and its decay) work; jumping asks where to land (see Jumping); stone to flesh, see Stone to flesh; polymorph zaps like the wand (see Polymorph); detected monsters stay shown in her monster memory until their cells are seen again (no `browse_map()`), and the blessed (Skilled) timed detection is not ported; force bolt, sleep and healing ask the zap directions after the energy is spent and go through the wand code (Int damage bonus, spell to-hit bonus, resisted at her level, healing blessed at Skilled), but a dismissed direction says "The magical energy is released!" and casts the spell at herself, since the port keeps no last direction; healing a monster cures no blindness (monsters are never blind); no exercise, Amulet or Wizard; confuse monster makes her hands glow red as the scroll does (1-2 charges, 2-9 at Skilled, one once she has 40; purple and more confusion if she is confused, which only a forced cast reaches), and each hand-to-hand hit on an unconfused monster uses a charge and confuses it unless it resists at her level (`nohandglow()`, "appears confused"); thrown and fired objects never do; `+` lists without reordering or sorting | No Amulet of Yendor, Wizard role or attribute exercise; the menu is the keyboard-free way; no stored `u.dx`/`u.dy` (ref: src/spell.c docast(), getspell(), rejectcasting(), spelleffects_check(), spell_backfire(), spelleffects(), cast_protection(), dovspell(), percent_success(); | Sokoban | soko1-1 .. soko4-2: each level has rolling boulder traps among its pits and holes, the level flags `noteleport`, `hardfloor` and `solidify`, non-diggable and non-passwall walls, `des.exclusion` regions that keep random monsters off the filled pits and holes, and on the top level two giant mimics posing as boulders, an Elbereth engraving and a cursed scroll of scare monster on the prize; the dungeon is `mazelike` and neutral; the zoo is an irregular room; `level_difficulty()` adds twice the levels climbed in a builds-up branch; cheating keeps a `sokocheat` conduct and solving a level and taking the prize are achievements (livelog); Sokoban's other rules cover Levitation, Flying, clinging, jumping, hurtling, polymorphing boulders, giants, steeds, the `m` prefix, rolling boulders and digging monsters | The eight maps, their stairs and branch stairs, the lit region, the locked and closed doors, every boulder, pit and hole, the two scrolls of earth on the entry level and the random food, ring and wand in the lua order; the rolling boulder traps on their spots, with no boulder (no launch spot in Sokoban: "Fortunately for you, no boulder was released."; harmless to monsters and no confirmation to step on one; a boulder pushed onto one rolls away); the top level's two giant mimics posing as boulders at random spots (m13 step 3, `LevelContent.appearAs`; 5.0's screening of a boulder disguise on a trap never runs, since `m->x` is already the mimic's square, so neither is it here); the level flags `sokoban`, `premapped`, `noteleport` (teleports are refused: see Teleportation), `hardfloor` (the entry level: digging down makes a pit), `mazelevel` and non-diggable walls and stone (a zap of digging: "The wall glows then fades."); pits and holes the hero digs are hers and do not count for solving; nothing phases; premapped as `premap_detect()` when made (every cell but stone, every boulder, every trap seen). Rules while a level has a pit or hole left: no diagonal pushes ("The boulder won't roll diagonally on this floor.", then the squeeze if she carries almost nothing), no diagonal squeeze between boulders and rock ("You cannot pass that way."; boulders count as rock for monsters' tight squeezes too), travel never through a boulder, pits and holes cannot be escaped ("Air currents pull you down into a pit!"; flying monsters are dragged in or yanked down), and a point of Luck for squeezing onto a boulder, breaking one or reading earth; filling the last pit or hole lifts them (spiked pits and trap doors do not count). Random new monsters keep off every trap square of such a level, in place of the exclusion regions. The top level's treasure zoo: a sleeping random monster on gold on each square of the region (the squares next to its first door stay empty, as for an irregular room), gold out of `500 * depth`, "Welcome to David's treasure zoo!" on the first entry (each monster wakes 1 in 3 unless she is stealthy), then an ordinary room; the prize (bag of holding 75% on soko1-1, 25% on soko1-2, else an amulet of reflection; not cursed) at one of its three spots, on a burned Elbereth (which guards no objects: `des.engraving()`'s default) under a cursed scroll of scare monster (m16 step 6); level difficulty is the depth | No teleporting, wall phasing, flight, conducts or achievements yet; the builds-up difficulty adjustment is not in `level_difficulty()` anywhere here (ref: dat/soko*.lua, dat/dungeon.lua, src/detect.c premap_detect(), src/hack.c moverock_core(), bad_rock(), cant_squeeze_thru(), test_move(), check_special_room(); src/trap.c sokoban_guilt(), maybe_finish_sokoban(), dotrap(), mintrap(); src/mkroom.c fill_zoo(); src/monmove.c m_avoid_soko_push_loc(); src/dungeon.c level_difficulty()) |

| Orcish Town | minetn-1: the town map with iron bars, `replace_terrain` holes in the shop walls, the desecrated shrine, the corpses of the priest, five shopkeepers and the Watch, 10-19 rocks each with a 90% boulder, the candles, lamp and empty wands, the orc horde and a teleport region; then stolen_booty(): a named gang (its leader and 5-14 orcs with gold, gems and shiny rings) migrates down the Mines, most of the town's orcs are named after it, and the booty (candles, keys, gloves, food, a sword) migrates to orcs below | The map, bars (a content terrain `nh:iron_bars`: blocks movement, not sight), holes, shrine, corpses, rocks and their boulders, candles, lamp, wands and horde, and the gang with its gold, gems and rings migrating to the deeper Mines levels. The teleport region keeps random arrivals out of the town (m15 step 5; its map-relative exclusion is cut at the map's last row). Left out: the gang's name and the naming of the town's orcs (with their rolls), and the booty items. A ring the hero has called "shiny" does not count as a shiny ring | No teleporting, monster names or object migration yet (ROADMAP) (ref: dat/minetn-1.lua; src/mkmaze.c stolen_booty(), migrate_orc(), shiny_orc_stuff()) |

(y/n, cancel = q) and then each line, every billed object (also one inside a container) is a line of its own, and "Pay whom?" is a menu of the shopkeepers in view; prices show in every object name (`doname_with_price()` everywhere, limited to the shop the hero is in as `get_cost_of_shop_item()` does); the door warning names her role | No artifacts, globs, dunce caps, Tourists or shirts; no loans (kickoff: no credit for gold); no getpos(); no player names (ref: src/shk.c getprice(), get_cost(), addtobill(), dopay(), pay_billed_items(), dopayobj(), u_left_shop(); src/objnam.c doname_base()) |

| Robbery and angry shopkeepers | Leaving a shop owing is robbery (`rob_shop()`): `call_kops()` sounds the alarm, angers the Watch and summons Keystone Kops (around the hero, by the down stairs and by the shop), dismissed when he is made happy; a shopkeeper paid off on another level migrates home and arrives near his door; `gf.followmsg` is an unsaved global; `shk_move()` also handles Conflict and displacement, and a shopkeeper following another customer says "I was looking for ..."; `make_angry_shk()` is called when he is teleported, falls or is dug out of his shop and when shop goods are broken from outside it; a scroll or spell of taming pacifies an angry shopkeeper (`tamedog()`) | Robbery as in NetHack (credit first, "You escaped the shop without paying!", "You stole N zorkmids worth of merchandise.", alignment, hot pursuit: the anger surcharge, following her to other levels), also when she steps onto the door of a sleeping or paralysed keeper and when she leaves the level from the shop (her shopkeeper next to her comes along); "An alarm sounds!" angers the Watch and (m18 step 6, kops.ts) brings the Keystone Kops: "The Keystone Kops appear!" around her when she stepped out of the door, else "The Keystone Kops are after you!" with a swarm near the down stairs and one near the shopkeeper (`makekops()`: `abs(depth) + rnd(5)` Kops, a third plus one sergeants, a sixth lieutenants, a ninth kaptains, gone kinds skipped); "But no one seems to respond to it." only with no Watch and every Kop kind gone; paying him off sends them away ("The Kops (disappointed) vanish into thin air.", silently when he arrives home from another level); `#pay` to an angry keeper: the robbed one's compensation, the 1000 gold appeasement (`rn2(3)`) and "Satisfied, Izchak suddenly disappears!", after which he is back on his spot at once and the Watch of his level calms down when the hero next arrives there; killing him frees his stock and voids her bill (`shkgone()`); attacking him while she is in no shop starts the pursuit; `tamedog()` only pacifies him, but nothing here can call it on him yet (food tames only domestic animals, no taming magic); `make_angry_shk()` is called when a wand of teleportation takes him out of his shop (no digging or shop damage yet); one hero, so no other customer | Levels persist in the state, so no migration is needed; the alarm is always heard (no Deaf); `remote_burglary()` has no caller (no telekinesis or grappling hook) (ref: src/shk.c u_left_shop(), rob_shop(), call_kops(), makekops(), kops_gone(), hot_pursuit(), make_happy_shk(), make_angry_shk(), dopay(), shk_move(), shkgone(); Traps live only on plain floor, so the teleporter waits in `modData.nh.vaultTele` while its corridor is secret and is made, unseen, after the action or turn that finds the corridor (searching, digging, detection); trap detection cannot show it before. studying it is never too hard, possible blind and never tears; `deadbook()` on the vibrating square performs the invocation with the primed Bell and Candelabrum, elsewhere a cursed one raises the dead (a master lich or nalfeshnee 1 time in 3, her corpses, `mkundead()`), a blessed one pacifies the undead she sees and tames co-aligned ones next to her, an uncursed one says one of three lines; a random engraving picks its rumor or engrave line with one `rn2(lines)` (C seeks a random byte of a padded file: uniform over lines too), and reading a rumor exercises nothing; walking, fighting, throwing, kicking, applying a polearm, every monster's turn and the turn's `!rn2(40 + 3 * Dex)` wear engravings, and land mines, rolling boulders onto mines, gushing fountains, swamps, drawbridges, the vault guard's corridor and walls, shop repairs and the water, lava and ice wishes delete them, as C; no axe digging, grappling hook, burial, digging up graves or cloud wish, and no special level has an engraving before its liquid cleanup.
